 145:                         .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN)
 146:                         .orElse(null);
 147:                 if (synchronizedPaper != null) {
 148:                     synchronizedPaper.setTakeProfit(newTarget);
 149:                     paperPositionRepository.save(synchronizedPaper);
 150:                 }
 151: 
 152:                 // FIX-053: Persist every TP revision so Dashboard can show the actual
 153:                 // Production management path instead of only the latest target value.
 154:                 positionManagementEventRepository.save(PositionManagementEvent.builder()
 155:                         .walletPositionId(managed.getId())
 156:                         .symbol(symbol)
 157:                         .eventType("TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED")
 158:                         .oldValueUsdt(oldTarget)
 159:                         .newValueUsdt(newTarget)
 160:                         .marketPriceUsdt(price)
 161:                         .reason(continuation.explanation())
 162:                         .occurredAt(changedAt)
 163:                         .build());
 164:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT extended: symbol={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, reason={}", symbol, oldTarget, newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 165:             } else if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 166:                     symbol, price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation())) {
 167:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), one);
 168:                 log.info("Live TAKE_PROFIT executed: symbol={}, price={}, target={}", symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 169:             }
 170:             return;
 171:         }
 172: 
 173:         // Stop loss is absolute and is never softened by continuation logic.
 174:         if (managed.getStopLossUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 175:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(
 176:                     symbol, price, "STOP_LOSS",
 177:                     "Live price " + price + " reached stop loss " + managed.getStopLossUsdt())) {
 178:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.STOPPED, "STOP_LOSS",
 179:                         "Live market price reached the configured stop loss.", one);
 180:                 log.info("Live STOP_LOSS executed: symbol={}, price={}, stop={}", symbol, price, managed.getStopLossUsdt());
 181:             }
 182:             return;
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation lock = dynamicProfitLockService.evaluatePrice(symbol, price);
 186: 
 187:         // FIX-118: during TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the superseded lock is intentionally
 188:         // non-executable, but earned profit is not left completely unprotected. Stop Loss
 189:         // has already had absolute priority above; this standalone floor is the next safety
 190:         // authority and does not depend on the old lock being triggered.
 191:         if (lock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 192:             String reason = lock.explanation();
 193:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}, target={}",
 194:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.hardProfitFloor(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 195:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 196:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 197:             }
 198:             return;
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         if (lock.active() || lock.triggered() || lock.rebasing()) {
 202:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_PATH] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, target={}, highest={}, active={}, " +
 203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 204:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(), lock.highestPrice(), lock.active(),
 205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
 206:         }
 207:         if (lock.triggered()) {
 208:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][LIVE_TRIGGER] positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, target={}",
 209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());
 210:             BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 211:             if (price.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0) {
 212:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " Hard protected-profit floor " + hardProfitFloor + " was breached.";
 213:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason)) {
 214:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", reason, one);
 215:                 }
 216:                 return;
 217:             }
 218: 
 219:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(one, five, hour);
 220:             if (lockDecision.exit()) {
 221:                 String reason = lock.explanation() + " " + lockDecision.explanation();
 222:                 if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, lockDecision.code(), reason)) {
 223:                     closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, lockDecision.code(), reason, one);
 224:                     log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK exit: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 225:                             symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.code());
 226:                 }
 227:                 return;
 228:             }
 229:             log.info("Live PROFIT_LOCK protected hold: symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, reason={}",
 230:                     symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lockDecision.explanation());
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         // Normal signal exits remain active even when Profit Lock is disabled. Higher-timeframe
 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 237:             if (walletAutoExecutionService.executeMechanicalExit(symbol, price, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation())) {
 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);
 239:                 log.info("Live signal-driven exit: symbol={}, price={}, reason={}", symbol, price, normalExit.code());
 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }
 245: 
 246:         // FIX-11T: Near-TP Failure Protection is deliberately LAST. TP/SL/profit-lock and
 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }
 250: 
 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionExitPolicy.java:1-140 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
   9:  *
  10:  * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
  11:  * higher-timeframe breakdown:
  12:  *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
  13:  *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
  14:  *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
  15:  *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
  16:  */
  17: @Component
  18: public class PositionExitPolicy {
  19: 
  20:     public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  21:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  22:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  23:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  24: 
  25:         boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
  26:                 && fiveMinute != null
  27:                 && !supportive(fiveDecision);
  28:         if (oneHourBreakdown) {
  29:             return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
  30:                     "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
  31:                             + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
  32:                             + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
  33:         }
  34: 
  35:         boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
  36:                 && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
  37:                 && bearish(currentDecision)
  38:                 && fiveMinute != null
  39:                 && bearish(fiveDecision)
  40:                 && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
  41:         if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
  42:             return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
  43:                     "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
  47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  56: 
  57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
  58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
  59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
  61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
  64:         }
  65: 
  66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
  67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
  68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
  69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
  70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
  72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
  75:         }
  76: 
  77:         if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
  78:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
  79:                     "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
  80:                             + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
  84:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
  85:                     "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
  86:         }
  87: 
  88:         return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
  89:                 "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
  90:                         + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  91:                         + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  92:                         + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
  96:         return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
 100:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
 101:                 || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
 102:                 || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 106:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 107:     }
 108: 
 109:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 110:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
 114:         return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
 118:         public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
 119:             return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
 120:         }
 121: 
 122:         public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
 123:             return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
 124:         }
 125: 
 126:         public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
 127:             return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
 128:         }
 129:     }
 130: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionContinuationPolicy.java:1-150 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * Shared production/replay policy for deciding whether a reached profit target should be extended.
  10:  *
  11:  * IMPORTANT: this class manages an ALREADY-OPEN winner only. It does not loosen BUY generation,
  12:  * entry validation, stop loss, or bearish timeframe vetoes.
  13:  */
  14: @Component
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class PositionContinuationPolicy {
  17:     /** Minor/moderate immutable-thesis deterioration is tolerated at a TP checkpoint. */
  18:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE = 2;
  19:     /** Momentum must remain essentially intact for the thesis-preservation continuation path. */
  20:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE = 1;
  21: 
  22:     private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;
  23: 
  24:     /**
  25:      * Backward-compatible evaluator used by older unit fixtures. Production/replay should pass
  26:      * the full immutable entry thesis through the overload below.
  27:      */
  28:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  29:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume) {
  30:         return evaluate(current, fiveMinute, oneHour,
  31:                 entryTrend, null, entryMomentum, entryVolume, null, null);
  32:     }
  33: 
  34:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  35:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryStructure,
  36:                                Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume,
  37:                                Integer entryConfidence, Integer entryTotal) {
  38:         if (current == null) return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú no fresh signal context.");
  39:         if (bearish(current.getDecision()) || (fiveMinute != null && bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision()))
  40:                 || (oneHour != null && bearish(oneHour.getDecision()))) {
  41:             return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú bearish timeframe detected; normal exit remains active.");
  42:         }
  43: 
  44:         boolean currentSupport = supportive(current.getDecision());
  45:         boolean fiveSupport = fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveMinute.getDecision());
  46:         boolean fiveNonBearish = fiveMinute != null && !bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision());
  47:         boolean oneSafe = oneHour == null || !bearish(oneHour.getDecision());
  48:         boolean oneBullish = oneHour != null && bullish(oneHour.getDecision());
  49: 
  50:         int trendFloor = Math.max(12, nvl(entryTrend) - 3);
  51:         int momentumFloor = Math.max(7, nvl(entryMomentum) - 4);
  52:         int volumeSoftFloor = Math.max(2, nvl(entryVolume) - 8);
  53: 
  54:         boolean trendHealthy = current.getTrendScore() >= trendFloor;
  55:         boolean momentumHealthy = current.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor;
  56:         boolean volumeSupportive = current.getVolumeScore() >= volumeSoftFloor;
  57: 
  58:         // Standard continuation: structure is still supportive. Volume is deliberately a soft
  59:         // confirmation because breakout volume commonly cools while a healthy trend continues.
  60:         boolean standard = currentSupport && fiveSupport && oneSafe
  61:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy
  62:                 && (volumeSupportive || oneBullish);
  63: 
  64:         // Protected HTF continuation: a live 1h BUY may carry a winner through a neutral/cooling
  65:         // 5m phase. It never overrides bearish context and still requires healthy 1m trend/momentum.
  66:         boolean htfProtected = currentSupport && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish
  67:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  68: 
  69:         // Healthy consolidation: low volume is deliberately NOT a hard veto for an already
  70:         // profitable position. If 1m is still WATCH/BUY, the higher timeframes are non-bearish,
  71:         // and trend/momentum remain healthy, cooling volume alone must not force a TP exit.
  72:         boolean healthyConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
  73:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  74: 
  75:         // HTF-supported consolidation: a strong 1h BUY may carry a winner through one neutral
  76:         // 1m candle when 5m remains non-bearish. This is position-management-only; supportive()
  77:         // is intentionally unchanged so entry rules are not loosened.
  78:         boolean currentNonBearish = !bearish(current.getDecision());
  79:         boolean htfSupportedConsolidation = currentNonBearish && !currentSupport
  80:                 && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  81: 
  82:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
  83:                 entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  84:                 entryConfidence, entryTotal, current);
  85: 
  86:         // FIX-011 / SOLUSDT 2026-08-19:
  87:         // At TP, SOL had 1m WATCH, 5m NEUTRAL, 1h WATCH and momentum improved 13->15.
  88:         // The old binary trend floor rejected continuation solely because trend fell 21->16
  89:         // (16 < 18), even though PositionManagementService scored the SAME immutable thesis as
  90:         // trend pressure 2/8, momentum pressure 0/5 and HOLD. This narrow path aligns both
  91:         // components: minor thesis cooling may extend a winner, but any bearish timeframe still
  92:         // vetoes above and genuine trend/momentum breakdown still fails these pressure limits.
  93:         boolean thesisIntactConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
  94:                 && thesis.trendPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE
  95:                 && thesis.momentumPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE;
  96: 
  97:         boolean extend = standard || htfProtected || healthyConsolidation
  98:                 || htfSupportedConsolidation || thesisIntactConsolidation;
  99:         String path = htfProtected ? "HTF_TREND"
 100:                 : (standard ? "STANDARD"
 101:                 : (htfSupportedConsolidation ? "HTF_SUPPORTED_CONSOLIDATION"
 102:                 : (healthyConsolidation ? "HEALTHY_CONSOLIDATION"
 103:                 : (thesisIntactConsolidation ? "THESIS_INTACT_CONSOLIDATION" : "NONE"))));
 104:         String checks = "current=" + decision(current)
 105:                 + ", 5m=" + decision(fiveMinute)
 106:                 + ", 1h=" + decision(oneHour)
 107:                 + ", trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + "/" + trendFloor
 108:                 + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + "/" + momentumFloor
 109:                 + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore() + "/soft " + volumeSoftFloor
 110:                 + ", thesisPressure=" + thesis.trendPressure() + "/8 trend, "
 111:                 + thesis.momentumPressure() + "/5 momentum";
 112: 
 113:         return new Evaluation(extend, extend
 114:                 ? "Continuation PASS (" + path + "): " + checks
 115:                     + ". Volume is advisory during a healthy winner; cooling volume alone does not end the trend."
 116:                 : "Continuation FAIL: " + checks
 117:                     + ". Trend/momentum or higher-timeframe structure no longer supports extending the target; allow normal protection/exit logic.");
 118:     }
 119: 
 120:     private int nvl(Integer v) { return v == null ? 0 : v; }
 121:     private String decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? "MISSING" : s.getDecision().name(); }
 122:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY || d == SignalDecision.WATCH; }
 123:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY; }
 124:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
 125:     public record Evaluation(boolean extendTarget, String explanation) {}
 126: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/DynamicProfitLockService.java:1-230 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletManagedPosition;
   5: import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletSettings;
   6: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
   7: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletSettingsRepository;
   8: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   9: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  11: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  12: 
  13: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  14: import java.math.RoundingMode;
  15: import java.time.Instant;
  16: import java.util.Locale;
  17: 
  18: /**
  19:  * Position-manager profit protection for already-open long positions.
  20:  *
  21:  * The BUY/SELL analysis engine is deliberately not involved here. The lock is
  22:  * derived only from the immutable entry price/take-profit stored on the wallet
  23:  * position and the best price observed after entry.
  24:  *
  25:  * Adaptive policy:
  26:  *  - derive an entry-quality score from the immutable entry signal score and confidence;
  27:  *  - stronger setups receive more room before protection activates;
  28:  *  - weaker/medium setups protect profits earlier;
  29:  *  - the protected price can only move upward and never loosens after activation.
  30:  *
  31:  * Administration values remain the fallback when entry-quality information is unavailable.
  32:  */
  33: @Service
  34: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  35: @Slf4j
  36: public class DynamicProfitLockService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  39:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  40: 
  41:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positionRepository;
  42:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  43:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  44: 
  45:     @Transactional
  46:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal signal) {
  47:         if (signal == null) {
  48:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  49:         }
  50:         return evaluatePrice(signal.getSymbol(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     /**
  54:      * Evaluates Dynamic Profit Lock directly from a live market price.
  55:      * This path is used by live position protection between candle-close signals.
  56:      *
  57:      * FIX-118 behavioral phase:
  58:      * TP_EXTENSION_REBASE preserves the historically earned lock in persistence but makes
  59:      * it non-executable until progress against the CURRENT TP reaches the configured
  60:      * activation threshold again. This gives an approved continuation meaningful room
  61:      * without forgetting that profit protection had already been earned.
  62:      */
  63:     @Transactional
  64:     public Evaluation evaluatePrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal currentPrice) {
  65:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || currentPrice == null
  66:                 || currentPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  67:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  68:         }
  69: 
  70:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  71:         WalletManagedPosition position = positionRepository
  72:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  73:                 .orElse(null);
  74:         if (position == null) {
  75:             return Evaluation.inactive("No open wallet position exists.");
  76:         }
  77: 
  78:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  79:         if (!settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled()) {
  80:             return Evaluation.inactive("Dynamic Profit Lock is disabled in Administration.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         BigDecimal entry = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
  84:         BigDecimal target = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
  85:         BigDecimal current = currentPrice;
  86:         if (entry == null || target == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  87:             return Evaluation.inactive("The position does not have a valid long take-profit distance.");
  88:         }
  89: 
  90:         ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings);
  91:         BigDecimal activation = profile.activationPercent();
  92:         BigDecimal initialLock = profile.initialLockPercent();
  93:         BigDecimal trailStep = profile.trailStepPercent();
  94:         BigDecimal previousHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
  95:         boolean previousPersistedActive = position.isProfitLockActive();
  96:         BigDecimal previousLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
  97:         BigDecimal previousProgress = position.getProfitLockProgressPercent();
  98:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
  99: 
 100:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest == null ? current : previousHighest.max(current);
 101:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(entry, target, highest);
 102:         BigDecimal lockPrice = previousLock;
 103:         ProfitLockState state = previousState;
 104:         boolean executableActive;
 105:         boolean triggered;
 106: 
 107:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 108:             // Keep the legacy active flag/lock persisted so history is not lost, but do not
 109:             // allow the superseded geometry to trigger an exit during the rebase window.
 110:             executableActive = false;
 111:             triggered = false;
 112:         } else {
 113:             // FIX-118 refinement: when a TP-extension rebase earns activation against the
 114:             // CURRENT target, rebuild the executable lock from the current geometry instead
 115:             // of carrying the historical pre-extension lock through ProfitLockPolicy's
 116:             // monotonic previous-lock rule.
 117:             boolean freshGeometryActivation = previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 118:             ProfitLockPolicy.State policyState = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 119:                     entry, target, current, highest,
 120:                     freshGeometryActivation ? false : (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || previousPersistedActive),
 121:                     freshGeometryActivation ? null : previousLock,
 122:                     true, activation, initialLock, trailStep);
 123:             highest = policyState.highestPrice();
 124:             progress = policyState.progressPercent();
 125:             lockPrice = policyState.lockPrice();
 126:             executableActive = policyState.active();
 127:             triggered = policyState.triggered();
 128:             state = executableActive ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 129:         }
 130: 
 131:         // During REBASE the legacy flag intentionally remains true: protection was historically
 132:         // earned. The explicit state is now the sole execution authority.
 133:         boolean persistedActive = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 134:                 ? true
 135:                 : executableActive;
 136: 
 137:         Instant activatedAt = position.getProfitLockActivatedAt();
 138:         if (persistedActive && activatedAt == null) activatedAt = Instant.now();
 139:         Instant rebaseStartedAt = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 140:                 ? position.getProfitLockRebaseStartedAt()
 141:                 : null;
 142: 
 143:         boolean changed = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 144:                 || highest.compareTo(position.getHighestPriceUsdt()) != 0
 145:                 || position.isProfitLockActive() != persistedActive
 146:                 || different(position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(), lockPrice)
 147:                 || different(position.getProfitLockProgressPercent(), progress)
 148:                 || position.getProfitLockState() != state
 149:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(position.getProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(), rebaseStartedAt)
 150:                 || (position.getProfitLockActivatedAt() == null && activatedAt != null);
 151: 
 152:         if (changed) {
 153:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(highest);
 154:             position.setProfitLockActive(persistedActive);
 155:             position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(lockPrice);
 156:             position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 157:             position.setProfitLockActivatedAt(activatedAt);
 158:             position.setProfitLockState(state);
 159:             position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(rebaseStartedAt);
 160:             position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 161:             positionRepository.save(position);
 162:         }
 163: 
 164:         BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = entry.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 165:         boolean hardFloorTriggered = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 166:                 && current.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0;
 167: 
 168:         if (changed || triggered || hardFloorTriggered || previousState != state) {
 169:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][PROFIT_LOCK_EVAL] positionId={}, symbol={}, current={}, entry={}, target={}, " +
 170:                             "previousHighest={}, highest={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousPersistedActive={}, persistedActive={}, " +
 171:                             "previousLock={}, lock={}, previousProgressPct={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, triggered={}, " +
 172:                             "hardFloor={}, hardFloorTriggered={}, persisted={}",
 173:                     position.getId(), symbol, current, entry, target,
 174:                     previousHighest, highest, previousState, state, previousPersistedActive, persistedActive,
 175:                     previousLock, lockPrice, previousProgress, progress, activation, triggered,
 176:                     hardProfitFloor, hardFloorTriggered, changed);
 177:         }
 178: 
 179:         String profileText = " Admin profile=" + profile.name() +
 180:                 " (activation=" + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%, initial lock=" +
 181:                 initialLock.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%, trail=" +
 182:                 trailStep.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%).";
 183:         String explanation;
 184:         if (hardFloorTriggered) {
 185:             explanation = "TP-extension rebase is active and price " + current
 186:                     + " breached the protected-profit safety floor " + hardProfitFloor + "." + profileText;
 187:         } else if (state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 188:             explanation = "Profit Lock is rebasing to the extended take-profit geometry; the historical lock "
 189:                     + lockPrice + " remains persisted but is non-executable. Current best progress is "
 190:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% and reactivation starts at "
 191:                     + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%." + profileText;
 192:         } else if (triggered) {
 193:             explanation = "Price " + current + " reached the protected profit-lock level " + lockPrice
 194:                     + " after the position had reached " + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 195:                     + "% of its take-profit distance." + profileText;
 196:         } else if (executableActive) {
 197:             explanation = "Profit Lock active at " + lockPrice + "; best progress is "
 198:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% of take-profit distance." + profileText;
 199:         } else {
 200:             explanation = "Profit Lock not active yet; best progress is "
 201:                     + progress.setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP) + "% and activation starts at "
 202:                     + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%." + profileText;
 203:         }
 204: 
 205:         return new Evaluation(true, executableActive, triggered, position.getId(), current, highest,
 206:                 progress, lockPrice, activation, state, hardProfitFloor, hardFloorTriggered, explanation);
 207:     }
 208: 
 209:     /**
 210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
 211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
 212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
 213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
 214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
 215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
 216:      */
 217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
 218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
 219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
 220:         }
 221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
 222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
 224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
 226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
 227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
 228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
 230:         }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/ProfitLockPolicy.java:1-120 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   4: 
   5: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   6: import java.math.RoundingMode;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * Pure Dynamic Profit Lock policy shared by live production and Proven/Regression.
  10:  * Keep all percentage/progression math here so replay can never drift from production.
  11:  */
  12: @Component
  13: public class ProfitLockPolicy {
  14:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  15:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  16: 
  17:     public State evaluate(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal current,
  18:                           BigDecimal previousHighest, boolean previouslyActive, BigDecimal previousLock,
  19:                           boolean enabled, BigDecimal activationPercent, BigDecimal initialLockPercent,
  20:                           BigDecimal trailStepPercent) {
  21:         if (entry == null || target == null || current == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  22:             return new State(previousHighest, previouslyActive, previousLock, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
  23:         }
  24: 
  25:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest;
  26:         if (highest == null || highest.compareTo(entry) < 0) highest = entry;
  27:         if (current.compareTo(highest) > 0) highest = current;
  28:         if (!enabled) return new State(highest, false, null, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
  29: 
  30:         BigDecimal distance = target.subtract(entry);
  31:         BigDecimal progress = highest.subtract(entry).multiply(HUNDRED)
  32:                 .divide(distance, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).max(BigDecimal.ZERO);
  33:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(activationPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
  34:         BigDecimal initial = nvl(initialLockPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(40));
  35:         BigDecimal step = nvl(trailStepPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(10));
  36: 
  37:         boolean active = previouslyActive;
  38:         BigDecimal lock = previousLock;
  39:         if (progress.compareTo(activation) >= 0) {
  40:             active = true;
  41:             BigDecimal completed = progress.subtract(activation).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
  42:                     .divide(step, 0, RoundingMode.DOWN);
  43:             BigDecimal lockedProgress = initial.add(completed.multiply(step));
  44:             BigDecimal maximum = progress.subtract(step).max(initial);
  45:             lockedProgress = lockedProgress.min(maximum).max(initial);
  46:             BigDecimal candidate = entry.add(distance.multiply(lockedProgress)
  47:                     .divide(HUNDRED, SCALE, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
  48:             candidate = candidate.max(entry.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005)));
  49:             if (lock == null || candidate.compareTo(lock) > 0) lock = candidate;
  50:         }
  51:         boolean triggered = active && lock != null && current.compareTo(lock) <= 0;
  52:         return new State(highest, active, lock, progress, triggered);
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private static BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) {
  56:         return value == null ? fallback : value;
  57:     }
  58: 
  59:     public record State(BigDecimal highestPrice, boolean active, BigDecimal lockPrice,
  60:                         BigDecimal progressPercent, boolean triggered) {}
  61: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionExitPolicy.java:1-150 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
   9:  *
  10:  * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
  11:  * higher-timeframe breakdown:
  12:  *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
  13:  *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
  14:  *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
  15:  *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
  16:  */
  17: @Component
  18: public class PositionExitPolicy {
  19: 
  20:     public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  21:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  22:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  23:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  24: 
  25:         boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
  26:                 && fiveMinute != null
  27:                 && !supportive(fiveDecision);
  28:         if (oneHourBreakdown) {
  29:             return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
  30:                     "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
  31:                             + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
  32:                             + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
  33:         }
  34: 
  35:         boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
  36:                 && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
  37:                 && bearish(currentDecision)
  38:                 && fiveMinute != null
  39:                 && bearish(fiveDecision)
  40:                 && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
  41:         if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
  42:             return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
  43:                     "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
  47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  56: 
  57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
  58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
  59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
  61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
  64:         }
  65: 
  66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
  67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
  68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
  69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
  70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
  72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
  75:         }
  76: 
  77:         if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
  78:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
  79:                     "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
  80:                             + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
  84:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
  85:                     "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
  86:         }
  87: 
  88:         return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
  89:                 "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
  90:                         + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  91:                         + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  92:                         + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
  96:         return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
 100:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
 101:                 || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
 102:                 || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 106:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 107:     }
 108: 
 109:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 110:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
 114:         return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
 118:         public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
 119:             return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
 120:         }
 121: 
 122:         public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
 123:             return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
 124:         }
 125: 
 126:         public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
 127:             return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
 128:         }
 129:     }
 130: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionContinuationPolicy.java:1-150 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * Shared production/replay policy for deciding whether a reached profit target should be extended.
  10:  *
  11:  * IMPORTANT: this class manages an ALREADY-OPEN winner only. It does not loosen BUY generation,
  12:  * entry validation, stop loss, or bearish timeframe vetoes.
  13:  */
  14: @Component
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class PositionContinuationPolicy {
  17:     /** Minor/moderate immutable-thesis deterioration is tolerated at a TP checkpoint. */
  18:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE = 2;
  19:     /** Momentum must remain essentially intact for the thesis-preservation continuation path. */
  20:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE = 1;
  21: 
  22:     private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;
  23: 
  24:     /**
  25:      * Backward-compatible evaluator used by older unit fixtures. Production/replay should pass
  26:      * the full immutable entry thesis through the overload below.
  27:      */
  28:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  29:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume) {
  30:         return evaluate(current, fiveMinute, oneHour,
  31:                 entryTrend, null, entryMomentum, entryVolume, null, null);
  32:     }
  33: 
  34:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  35:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryStructure,
  36:                                Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume,
  37:                                Integer entryConfidence, Integer entryTotal) {
  38:         if (current == null) return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú no fresh signal context.");
  39:         if (bearish(current.getDecision()) || (fiveMinute != null && bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision()))
  40:                 || (oneHour != null && bearish(oneHour.getDecision()))) {
  41:             return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú bearish timeframe detected; normal exit remains active.");
  42:         }
  43: 
  44:         boolean currentSupport = supportive(current.getDecision());
  45:         boolean fiveSupport = fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveMinute.getDecision());
  46:         boolean fiveNonBearish = fiveMinute != null && !bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision());
  47:         boolean oneSafe = oneHour == null || !bearish(oneHour.getDecision());
  48:         boolean oneBullish = oneHour != null && bullish(oneHour.getDecision());
  49: 
  50:         int trendFloor = Math.max(12, nvl(entryTrend) - 3);
  51:         int momentumFloor = Math.max(7, nvl(entryMomentum) - 4);
  52:         int volumeSoftFloor = Math.max(2, nvl(entryVolume) - 8);
  53: 
  54:         boolean trendHealthy = current.getTrendScore() >= trendFloor;
  55:         boolean momentumHealthy = current.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor;
  56:         boolean volumeSupportive = current.getVolumeScore() >= volumeSoftFloor;
  57: 
  58:         // Standard continuation: structure is still supportive. Volume is deliberately a soft
  59:         // confirmation because breakout volume commonly cools while a healthy trend continues.
  60:         boolean standard = currentSupport && fiveSupport && oneSafe
  61:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy
  62:                 && (volumeSupportive || oneBullish);
  63: 
  64:         // Protected HTF continuation: a live 1h BUY may carry a winner through a neutral/cooling
  65:         // 5m phase. It never overrides bearish context and still requires healthy 1m trend/momentum.
  66:         boolean htfProtected = currentSupport && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish
  67:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  68: 
  69:         // Healthy consolidation: low volume is deliberately NOT a hard veto for an already
  70:         // profitable position. If 1m is still WATCH/BUY, the higher timeframes are non-bearish,
  71:         // and trend/momentum remain healthy, cooling volume alone must not force a TP exit.
  72:         boolean healthyConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
  73:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  74: 
  75:         // HTF-supported consolidation: a strong 1h BUY may carry a winner through one neutral
  76:         // 1m candle when 5m remains non-bearish. This is position-management-only; supportive()
  77:         // is intentionally unchanged so entry rules are not loosened.
  78:         boolean currentNonBearish = !bearish(current.getDecision());
  79:         boolean htfSupportedConsolidation = currentNonBearish && !currentSupport
  80:                 && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  81: 
  82:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
  83:                 entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  84:                 entryConfidence, entryTotal, current);
  85: 
  86:         // FIX-011 / SOLUSDT 2026-08-19:
  87:         // At TP, SOL had 1m WATCH, 5m NEUTRAL, 1h WATCH and momentum improved 13->15.
  88:         // The old binary trend floor rejected continuation solely because trend fell 21->16
  89:         // (16 < 18), even though PositionManagementService scored the SAME immutable thesis as
  90:         // trend pressure 2/8, momentum pressure 0/5 and HOLD. This narrow path aligns both
  91:         // components: minor thesis cooling may extend a winner, but any bearish timeframe still
  92:         // vetoes above and genuine trend/momentum breakdown still fails these pressure limits.
  93:         boolean thesisIntactConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
  94:                 && thesis.trendPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE
  95:                 && thesis.momentumPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE;
  96: 
  97:         boolean extend = standard || htfProtected || healthyConsolidation
  98:                 || htfSupportedConsolidation || thesisIntactConsolidation;
  99:         String path = htfProtected ? "HTF_TREND"
 100:                 : (standard ? "STANDARD"
 101:                 : (htfSupportedConsolidation ? "HTF_SUPPORTED_CONSOLIDATION"
 102:                 : (healthyConsolidation ? "HEALTHY_CONSOLIDATION"
 103:                 : (thesisIntactConsolidation ? "THESIS_INTACT_CONSOLIDATION" : "NONE"))));
 104:         String checks = "current=" + decision(current)
 105:                 + ", 5m=" + decision(fiveMinute)
 106:                 + ", 1h=" + decision(oneHour)
 107:                 + ", trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + "/" + trendFloor
 108:                 + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + "/" + momentumFloor
 109:                 + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore() + "/soft " + volumeSoftFloor
 110:                 + ", thesisPressure=" + thesis.trendPressure() + "/8 trend, "
 111:                 + thesis.momentumPressure() + "/5 momentum";
 112: 
 113:         return new Evaluation(extend, extend
 114:                 ? "Continuation PASS (" + path + "): " + checks
 115:                     + ". Volume is advisory during a healthy winner; cooling volume alone does not end the trend."
 116:                 : "Continuation FAIL: " + checks
 117:                     + ". Trend/momentum or higher-timeframe structure no longer supports extending the target; allow normal protection/exit logic.");
 118:     }
 119: 
 120:     private int nvl(Integer v) { return v == null ? 0 : v; }
 121:     private String decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? "MISSING" : s.getDecision().name(); }
 122:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY || d == SignalDecision.WATCH; }
 123:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY; }
 124:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
 125:     public record Evaluation(boolean extendTarget, String explanation) {}
 126: }

  35:     public static final String DIRECT_SOURCE="BINANCE_KLINE_DIRECT_FIX153";
  36:     public static BigDecimal normalizePrice(BigDecimal price){
  37:         return price==null?null:price.setScale(PRICE_SCALE,java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);

  39:     private Instant referenceNow(){Instant local=Instant.now();return exchangeClock==null?local:exchangeClock.correctJvm(local);}
  40:     public String invalidReason(BigDecimal price,Instant observed,Instant now){
  41:         if(price==null || price.signum()<=0)return "INVALID_PRICE";
  42:         if(observed==null)return "MISSING_TIMESTAMP";
  43:         if(observed.isAfter(now.plusSeconds(futureSkew)))return "FUTURE_TIMESTAMP";
  44:         if(observed.isBefore(now.minusSeconds(maxAge)))return "STALE_PRICE";
  45:         return null;
  46:     }
  47:     /** FIX-153: called in INPUT transaction. Invalid observations never occupy slot 1.
  48:      * A duplicate-key error is classified only if the exact canonical key exists; other
  49:      * duplicate constraints/errors propagate. JDBC statement errors do not poison a JPA proxy TX. */
  50:     public Long recordPending(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observed){

  52:         Instant now=referenceNow();String invalid=invalidReason(normalizedPrice,observed,now);

  55:             Long ageMs=observed==null?null:java.time.Duration.between(observed,now).toMillis();
  56:             log.info("[FIX-153][PRICE_NOT_CANONICAL] symbol={}, price={}, observedUtc={}, observationAgeMs={}, reason={}",symbol,normalizedPrice,observed,ageMs,invalid);
  57:             return null;
  58:         }
  59:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder keys=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();

  77:     public boolean laterApplied(String symbol,Instant observed){

  79:     }
  80:     /** Exact event lineage prevents a caller from completing a different receipt.
  81:      * A lost resolver race rolls back the entire effects transaction. Slot 1 is retained. */
  82:     public void finish(long id,String symbol,Instant observed,BigDecimal price,String state,String reason){
  83:         price=normalizePrice(price);
  84:         if(!org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
  85:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome requires effects transaction");
  86:         if(!java.util.Set.of("APPLIED","NON_CANONICAL").contains(state))throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 invalid effects outcome");
  87:         int n=jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE market_price_event SET delivery_status=?,outcome_reason=? WHERE id=? AND source=? AND symbol=? AND observed_at=? AND price=? AND ordering_slot=1 AND delivery_status='PENDING'",state,reason,id,DIRECT_SOURCE,symbol,Timestamp.from(observed),price);
  88:         if(n!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 price outcome ownership/lineage changed: event="+id);
  89:         org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization(){

  96:             }

 470:     /**
 471:      * Executes a mechanical live-price exit (TP / SL / Profit Lock) without requiring
 472:      * a newly generated trade signal. The open wallet position is the source of truth.
 473:      */
 474:     @Transactional
 475:     public boolean executeMechanicalExit(
 476:             String symbol,
 477:             BigDecimal executionPrice,
 478:             String executionReason,
 479:             String executionMessage
 480:     ) {
 481:         String pair = normalizePair(symbol);
 482:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
 483:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
 484:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
 485:                 .orElse(null);
 486:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
 487:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
 488:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
 489: 
 490:         if (position == null || position.getId() == null || position.getQuantity() == null
 491:                 || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) {
 492:             return false;
 493:         }
 494: 
 495:         String reason = executionReason == null || executionReason.isBlank()
 496:                 ? "MECHANICAL_EXIT"
 497:                 : executionReason.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 498:         String key = "POSITION:" + position.getId() + ":" + reason;
 499:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
 500: 
 501:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 502:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 503:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 504:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
 505:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return false;
 506: 
 507:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
 508:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 509:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 510:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 511:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 512:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 513:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 514: 
 515:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
 516:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 517:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 518:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
 519:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
 520:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
 521:         }
 522:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
 523:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
 524:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
 525: 
 526:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
 527:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
 528:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
 529:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");
 530:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 531:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
 532: 
 533:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
 534:                 .executionKey(key)
 535:                 .symbol(pair)
 536:                 .side("SELL")
 537:                 .quantity(quantity)
 538:                 .priceUsdt(price)
 539:                 .grossAmountUsdt(gross)
 540:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
 541:                 .netAmountUsdt(gross)
 542:                 .costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
 543:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized)
 544:                 .realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
 545:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
 546:                 .executionReason(reason)
 547:                 .status("EXECUTED")
 548:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
 549:                 .notes("Mechanical live-price position exit")
 550:                 .executionMessage(executionMessage == null ? reason + " triggered at " + price : executionMessage)
 551:                 .build());
 552: 
 553:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
 554:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
 555:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
 556:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 557:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 558:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 559:                 });
 560:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 561:         return true;
 562:     }

 565:      * FIX-11T: execute one idempotent 50% Near-TP partial harvest against the currently

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:100-200 =====
 100:     @Transactional(propagation = org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Propagation.NEVER)
 101:     public Optional<PaperPosition> processSharedSignal(TradeSignal signal, long sourceEventId) {
 102:         return processingCoordinator.process(signal.getId(),false,this::processSignalBody,sourceEventId);
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     /** FIX-127: existing decision order; invoked inside the coordinator's transaction. */
 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {
 108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
 109:         }
 110: 
 111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
 112: 
 113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
 114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
 115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
 116:             try {
 117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
 118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
 120:             }
 121:         }

 123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);

 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
 572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
 573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
 577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Scope is only SHIBUSDT BUY 106 / SELL 107, paper position 35, managed position 64, 1m signal 89469 and 5m trigger 89473—not the later 90192/65 trade. Entry was `2026-10-09T20:45:05.997Z` / `23:45:05.997 KSA`; exit was `2026-10-09T20:58:43.554Z` / `23:58:43.554 KSA`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q1:** The position exited through the profit-lock branch because protection received `0.000005420000`, below its active lock `0.000005421865`. It was also below TP `0.000005433730`, so the TP branch was not entered.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The lock armed on accepted event 1085348 at `2026-10-09T20:58:09.444Z` / `23:58:09.444 KSA`, when price was `0.000005430000`, or 84.2815% of the original TP distance. The next relevant accepted price, event 1085485, was `0.000005420000` at `20:58:43.449Z` / `23:58:43.449 KSA`; it breached the lock and produced SELL 107 105 ms later.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** At the breach, the policy saw 1m `WATCH` 89795, 5m `WATCH` 89724, and 1h `NEUTRAL` 88327. A profit-lock hold requires either bullish 1h plus supportive 5m, or 1h `WATCH` plus supportive 5m. `NEUTRAL` matches neither, so the implemented default is `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q2:** Target continuation is evaluated only after price reaches the live managed TP. It was never reached by the evidenced protection path, and the position was already closed about 39 minutes before the preserved `2026-10-09T21:38:00Z` / `2026-10-10T00:38:00 KSA` later candle. There was therefore no open position to extend.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Q3:** Protection did re-evaluate each accepted live price. At the lock breach, however, it does not assess remaining target distance, momentum, or the advisory output; it invokes `PositionExitPolicy` once using the three latest signal labels, then sells if that policy returns exit. Later upside was not information available at the breach.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** On the recorded `0.00000001` price increments, TP `0.000005433730` required a received price of at least `0.000005440000`. The highest accepted protection price evidenced for this position was `0.000005430000`.

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** “TP was never reached” must be limited to system evidence: no accepted or logged-rejected TP-capable SHIB observation was preserved. Binance could theoretically have traded above TP between kline-close observations, because protection consumes kline field `c`, not intrabar high/low: [BinanceKlineService.java](</C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:91>).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The breach policy reads the latest stored labels without validating their freshness or data quality. The hourly `NEUTRAL` signal was the newest closed hourly result, but about 58 minutes old at exit. This is an implemented-rule observation, not evidence that the trade violated that rule.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The TP-continuation policy can accept non-bearish 1h context, while the breached-lock hold requires at least 1h `WATCH`. Thus a hypothetical TP touch could have been eligible for continuation under different score inputs, while the actual lock breach had to exit. The position never reached that checkpoint, and the required contemporaneous score inputs for that counterfactual are not fully preserved.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The later rise does not show that a hold would have been better. A policy change would need fee/slippage-aware, out-of-sample testing with intrabar ordering, and cannot rely on this single trade.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No immutable deployed-binary hash or historical configuration snapshot proves current source was the exact running artifact; matching logs and persisted behavior corroborate it.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No Replay was run. Source inspection also shows Replay differs by mode: exact-price Replay relies on persisted price events, while fallback Replay evaluates signal prices and exit rules differently: [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](</C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:183>) and [line 562](</C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:562>).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The preserved later-candle evidence is for `00:38 KSA`. If “12:38 KSA” meant noon, that exact candle is outside this evidence bundle; the position was nevertheless already closed from `23:58:43 KSA`.

VERDICT: APPROVED
